      let a_2 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_2, i_1)) in ((0 <= i_2) ->

  Have: P_safe_position(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0, col_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_9' *)
  Have: 0 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      (Mint_0[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i))] = 0))).
  (* Invariant 'i_37' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((i_1 < n) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      ((row_0 < i_1) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) != x_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_38' *)
  Have: x_1 < (n * x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_43' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, row_0, i) < x_4))).
  (* Invariant 'i_44' *)
  Have: forall i_2,i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 < n) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i <= n) ->
      (L_idx(n, i_2, i_1) != L_idx(n, row_0, i)))))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_45' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) != x_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_48' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((i_1 < n) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      ((row_0 < i_1) -> (x_4 <= L_idx(n, i_1, i)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_58' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) < x_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_61' *)
  Have: 0 < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_9' *)
  Have: (0 <= j) /\ (j <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_11' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_1, n, board_0, x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: P_safe_position(a_1, n, board_0, row_0, col_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z.
      let a_3 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_1, i)) in ((0 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      (a_1[a_3] = Mint_0[a_3]))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < j) ->

       (if (i = col_0) then 1 else 0)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)

      (a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i))] = 0))).
  (* Invariant 'i_45' *)

      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) != x))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_58' *)

      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) < x))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n <= j.
}

      P_binary_board(a_5, n, board_0) /\

      P_rows_empty_from(a_5, n, board_0, row_0) /\
      P_no_attack_prefix(a_5, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: (-1) <= col_0.
}

Let a = shift_sint32(board_0, x).

Let a_2 = memcpy(a_1, a, Mint_undef_1, a, n).
Let x_3 = 1 + row_0.
Let x_4 = n * x_3.
Let a_3 = shift_sint32(board_0, x_4).
Let x_5 = x_1 - x_4.
Let a_4 = memcpy(a_2, a_3, Mint_undef_2, a_3, x_5).
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(col_0) /\ is_sint32(n) /\ is_sint32(row_0) /\
      is_sint32(solve_from_row_0).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(board_0.base) <= 0) /\ linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Goal *)
  When: !invalid(Malloc_0, a_3, x_5).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_valid_board(Malloc_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: (row_0 <= n) /\ (0 <= row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_binary_board(Mint_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Else *)
  Have: row_0 != n.
  (* Invariant 'i_29' *)
  Have: row_0 < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: 0 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: (0 <= col_0) /\ (col_0 <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_31' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_32' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_33' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_34' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Then *)
  Have: col_0 < n.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: (n + x) <= x_1.
  (* Call 'place_queen' *)

      P_binary_board(a_2, n, board_0) /\
      P_rows_solved(a_2, n, board_0, x_3) /\
      P_rows_empty_from(a_2, n, board_0, x_3) /\
      P_no_attack_prefix(a_2, n, board_0, x_3).
  (* Assertion *)

  (* Call 'solve_from_row' *)

      (((solve_from_row_0 = 0) -> P_binary_board(a_4, n, board_0))) /\

  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Else *)
  Have: row_0 != n.
  (* Invariant 'i_29' *)
  Have: row_0 < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: 0 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
  Have: (0 <= col_0) /\ (col_0 <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_31' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_32' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_33' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_34' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Then *)
  Have: col_0 < n.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: (n + x) <= x_1.
  (* Call 'place_queen' *)
  Have: P_safe_position(a_1, n, board_0, row_0, col_0) /\
      P_binary_board(a_2, n, board_0) /\
      P_rows_solved(a_2, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_rows_empty_from(a_2, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_no_attack_prefix(a_2, n, board_0, x_2).
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: x_3 <= x_1.
  (* Call 'solve_from_row' *)
  Have: ((-1) <= row_0) /\ P_binary_board(a_4, n, board_0) /\
      P_rows_solved(a_4, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_rows_empty_from(a_4, n, board_0, x_2) /\
      P_no_attack_prefix(a_4, n, board_0, x_2).
}
Prove: P_rows_solved(a_4, n, board_0, row_0).
Prover Alt-Ergo 2.6.3 returns Valid (Qed:13ms) (40ms) (4687)
Prover CVC5 1.1.2 returns Valid (Qed:13ms) (47ms) (29818)
Prover Z3 4.13.3 returns Valid (Qed:13ms) (12ms) (88425)
